Soft objective function:
```
def soft_obj_and_grad(p, tau):
    b = conv_full(p)
    bmax = b.max()
    shifted = b - bmax
    exp = np.exp(shifted/tau)
    Z = exp.sum()
    q = exp / Z
    obj = tau * (np.log(Z) + bmax/tau) # = bmax + tau log sum exp(shifted/tau)
    g = 2 * corr_valid(q,p)
    return obj, g, b, bmax
```

Now design optimizers in detail.

FFT-based computations:
```
def convolve_full(a):
    n = len(a)
    if n <= 2048: return np.convolve(a,a)
    L = next_fast_len(2*n-1)
    fa = scipy.fft.rfft(a, L)
    b = scipy.fft.irfft(fa*fa, L)[:2*n-1]
    # remove tiny imaginary? irfft real
    # Due to roundoff, small negative possible; for max we can set b = np.maximum(b, 0)
    return b
```
Using scipy.fft; if import fails, fallback numpy. We can implement `next_fast_len` from scipy.fft import next_fast_len. To avoid per-call overhead, maybe choose power of two: L=1; while L<2*n-1: L*=2. scipy.fft.next_fast_len optimized. Use global? Function call overhead okay. Could cache? Many n same. We'll just call.

`corr_valid(w,p)`: w length 2n-1, p length n. Return length n: c_i=sum_{j=0}^{n-1} w[i+j] p[j]. Use q valid. Implement direct for small: `np.correlate(w, p, 'valid')`. For FFT:
```
def corr_valid_fft(w,p):
    n=p.size
    if n <= 2048: return np.correlate(w,p,'valid')
    L=next_fast_len(3*n)  # full corr length M+N-1 = 3n-2
    W = scipy.fft.rfft(w, L)
    P = scipy.fft.rfft(p, L)
    corr = scipy.fft.irfft(W * np.conj(P), L)[:n]
    return corr
```
Need confirm for q length M=2n-1,N=n, valid length n = first n of corr. Yes.
Potential numerical error: corr can be slightly negative; okay.

For direct, np.correlate of length up to 2048 O4M ok.

Objective surrogate:
```
def compute_b_and_weights(p, tau_rel, lam, mean):
    b=convolution(p)
    c = b - mean
    std = sqrt(max(sum(c*c)/(len(b)), 1e-24))
    z = c / (tau_rel*std)
    z_shift = z - z.max()
    expz = np.exp(z_shift)
    q = expz / expz.sum()
    w = c / std + lam * q
    # Objective F = 0.5*sum((c/std)^2) + lam*tau_rel*std*logsumexp(c/(tau*std)) maybe zbase = bmax/(tau std)?
    # logsumexp(c/(tau std)) = bmax/(tau std) + log(sum exp(z_shift))? Since using c not b. If c can negative. For objective threshold relative mean. 
    lse = (c.max()/(tau_rel*std)) + np.log(expz.sum())
    F = 0.5*np.sum((c/std)**2) + lam*tau_rel*std*lse
    return b, w, F
```
But if c centered, soft on c not b; max of c ~ M-mean. If c has negative low, lse might be not approximating max b but max c (same). Good. However, q weights low? Softmax on c can include moderately lower but not lowest. Fine. If tau_rel large, q approx uniform; low not strongly. L2 handles lows.
Mean: Since sum b = (sum p)^2 might drift due to projection errors; use current sum? We normalize p to S; sum b exact maybe slight. Use mean = sum(b)/len(b). For target average, use current sum(b)/m. ```
def optimize_adam(p0, budget, phases):
    p = normalize(p0)
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t=0
    lr_start=0.1; lr_min=0.005; beta1=.9 beta2=.999
    best=...
    for phase in phases:
       tau_rel, lam, target? 
       while time<phase_end:
          b=conv(p); c=b-b.mean(); std=...
          q=softmax(c/(tau*std))
          w = c/std + lam*q*Kscale? 
          grad = 2*corr(w,p)
          Adam update.
          p=project(p - step)
          if each 50 compute score exact; update best.
```

Should we normalize gradient raw by its norm? Adam normalizes per coordinate by sqrt(v), so not. But if a coordinate has huge gradient due many variables, step capped at lr. Good. So Adam robust. We'll implement:
```
def adam_project_step(p, grad, lr, m, v, t):
    m = β1*m + (1-β1)*grad
    v = β2*v + (1-β2)*grad*grad
    mhat = m/(1-β1**t)
    vhat = v/(1-β2**t)
    update = lr * mhat / (sqrt(vhat)+eps)
    p_new = project_simplex_sum(p - update, S)
    return p_new, m,v
```
Here grad raw maybe O(S). v ~ S^2; mhat/sqrt(v) ~ sign. lr absolute step. p entries scale S/n. lr=0.05 relative p entries? For n=3000, p avg0.025, step 0.05 can zero many; projection may distribute. Now adaptivity / line search:
Using Adam with raw direction, step may oscillate. We can include simple backtracking over score? Since we compute b each iteration, after candidate p_new, we could compute its b to evaluate? But optimizer already computes b for current p; to evaluate candidate, need conv candidate before gradient next? We can integrate: At iteration k, compute current b and grad. Generate candidate p_new via Adam. Before commit, compute candidate b and score. If candidate score < current score * (1+tol) or surrogate improved, accept; else reduce local lr and retry. This doubles conv cost but improves. We can limit to when n not too large or a fraction. However, candidate b can be used as current b next iteration, saving compute. We also need gradient for candidate, so must compute its weights anyway. So we could implement line search by computing candidate bmultiple times. But time. We can do every attempt with up to 3 backtracks. For n=3000, okay; n=10000 maybe too. We can condition if n <= 10000.

Pseudo:
```
lr = base_lr
state m,v
while time:
  b = conv(p)
  score = ...
  update best
  w = weights(b,tau,alpha)
  grad = 2*corr(w,p)
  ok=False
  for scale in [1,0.5,0.25,0.125]:
     p_try = adam_step(p,grad,scale*lr,...)
     if time...
     b_try=conv(p_try); sc_try=score
     # Accept if sc_try <= sc + 1e-9 or surrogate improved
     if sc_try <= best_score + ... maybe? Since nonmonotonic; better accept if sc_try < sc*1.001 and not much worse
        if sc_try < best_score: best=p_try
        p=p_try; b=b_try; ok=True
        # lr maybe increase slightly if improved a lot
     else: continue
  if not ok:
     lr=max(lr_min, lr*0.5)
     # If several failures, restart from best with perturbation or reduce lr
```
But computing p_try with Adam: If we retry with different scale, m,v should correspond to actual accepted step, not used? If we update m,v before trial, need copy. Could avoid line search with Adam; simply accept and if score worsens, reduce lr and maybe revert p. Simpler: no line search; maintain epsilon. Given time, line search may complicate.

Maybe implement greedy without line search but with restart: run Adam until no improvement for `stagnation` iters, then if score not best, p=best plus small random; lr reset. Since we track best, transient increases okay. Use Adam with projection; often works.

But Adam can get stuck in flat high max; periodic random perturbation helps. Use multi-start.

Let's design `adam_run(p0, t_budget, tag, tracking_best)`:
- n = len(p0); S=sqrt(2n)
- p = normalize copy.
- m,v zeros; t=0; lr=0.1*S/n? Let's choose initial `lr0 = 0.25 * S / n * 10?` We need pick. Since p avg=S/n. Adam step ~lr. For p avg 0.025, lr 0.01 =0.4avg. Let's set lr0 = 0.5 * S / n (0.0125). Min lr=0.001*S/n? maybe 1e-5.
- momentum beta1=0.8, beta2=0.95.
- Stagnation: Keep last improvement step. If no improvement for 200 iterations, reduce lr; if no improvement for 1000, perturb: p = best + gaussian*epsilon, projection. Epsilon = 0.05*std(p) maybe.
- Tau schedule within run: f = elapsed/budget; tau_rel = max(0.01, 0.4*(1-f)+0.01). alpha_soft = 0.1 + f*0.9. alpha_l2 = 1.0 - 0.5*f? Keep 0.5.
- Weights:
   c=b-b.mean(); norm_c = l2(c). if norm_c > 1e-12: u_l2=c/norm_c else 0.
   z = c/(norm_c? or std*tau? If using norm_c, z=c/tau_abs where tau_abs=tau_rel*norm_c? This makes tau relative to L2 norm. q=softmax(c/(tau_rel*norm_c)). Norm_c ~√m*std. If tau_rel=0.1, tau_abs~7.7; c values ~0.3; z small ~0.04 => uniform. Too large. Use std not norm. Better tau_abs = tau_rel * max(1e-12, std) where std = norm_c/√m. Then z=c/(tau_rel std). tau_rel=0.1 => z max ~ (1)/(0.1)=10, top active. For broad tau_rel=1 => z max1. Good. So use std.
   qsum; if qsum zero, q=zeros; unit_soft = q / norm(q) if norm>0.
   w = alpha_l2*u_l2 + alpha_soft*unit_soft.
   # Optionally ensure w not all zero: if norm(w)<1e-12: w=u_l2.
- Gradient `g = corr(w,p)`? Derivative 2corr but scale factor omitted; use `g = corr(w,p)` or 2. Sign same. Adam cancels scale. Use no 2.
- Adam update:
```
t +=1
m = b1*m + (1-b1)*g
v = b2*v + (1-b2)*g*g
mhat = m/(1-b1**t)
vhat = v/(1-b2**t)
step = lr * mhat / (np.sqrt(vhat)+1e-8)
p_raw = p - step
p_new = project_simplex_sum(p_raw, S)
# optional clip p_new = np.maximum(p_new,0)
```

    v=v.astype(float)

Sort method O(n log n) in C might be faster. Let's implement standard fast projection using sort:
```
def project_simplex_sum(v,S):
    n=v.size
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv=np.cumsum(u)
    rho=np.nonzero(u * np.arange(1,n+1) > (cssv - S))[0][-1]
    tau=(cssv[rho]-S)/(rho+1)
    return np.maximum(v-tau,0)
```
This is O(n log n) but sort in C optimized. For many iterations, sorting 10k 10000 times maybe heavy. Bisection may be faster for large n? Could use selection?    tau? not used. K as above; alpha.

Initialization functions:
```
def make_uniform(n): return np.full(n,1.0)

def make_edge_power(n, aL, aR):
    x = np.arange(1,n+1)
    left = x
    right = n - x + 1
    val = left**(-aL) * right**(-aR)
    if aL<0? We'll use positive exponents -a => left**(-aL). Actually value = (1/left)^aL * (1/right)^aR. Use exp(-aL*np.log(left)).
    return np.maximum(val,1e-12)
```
For aL=0.5, right=0.5: edge-heavy. For aL=0, aR=0 uniform.
Also singular asymmetric: aL=1,aR=0.2 etc.

Sine init:
```
def make_sinusoidal(n, coeffs):
    t=np.arange(n)/n
    val=1 + a0*np.sin(2πt)+a1*np.sin(4πt)+b0*np.cos(2πt)+b1*np.cos(4πt)
    val=np.maximum(val,1e-8)
```
But we need no closures; just function with parameters.

Interpolate provided:
```
def resample(arr,n):
    if len(arr)==n: return arr.copy()
    x_old=np.linspace(0,1,len(arr)); x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    return np.maximum(np.interp(x_new,x_old,arr),0.0)
```

   arr=np.array(seq,float)
   n=arr.size
   eps = 1e-9 * max(1.0, arr.mean())

Implementing LP in code:
```
from scipy.optimize import linprog
def lp_direction(p, K, upper_factor):
    n=p.size; b=conv(p); M=b.max()
    A_idx = top K
    # build dense A_ub K x n
    A = np.zeros((K,n), float)
    for r,k in enumerate(A_idx):
       lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
       # columns j lo..hi coefficient p[k-j]
       # coefficient vector p[k-hi : k-lo+1][::-1]?
       # for j in lo..hi: coeff=p[k-j]
       coeffs = p[k-hi : k-lo+1][::-1]
       A[r, lo:hi+1] = coeffs
    bounds = [(0, upper_factor*S/n) for _ in range(n)]  # tuple list large; use (0, ub scalar)
    res=linprog(c=-np.ones(n), A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(K,M), bounds=(0, ub), method='highs')
    if res.success: return res.x
```
This loops K up to 100, assigning slices; O(K*n) memory? A dense Kx n, for K=50,n=3000 150k good. For K=200,n=8000 1.6M 13MB; okay. Building in Python loops maybe okay few times.
Bounds scalar `(0, ub)` in linprog applies to all variables. Use ub = 10*S/n? But if variable needs >ub, constrained too much. We can set ub = 100*S/n or no upper? Unbounded risk. Let's detail `lp_update_sequence`:
```
def lp_update_sequence(p0, seconds, end_time, best_seq, best_score, K=40, max_rounds=8):
    n=p0.size; S=sqrt(2n)
    p=normalize_to_sum(p0,S); best local
    start=time
    # cooldown for first 10s? 
    for round in range(max_rounds):
       if time>=end: break
       b=conv_full(p); sc=local_score(p,b); update best
       M=b.max()
       # choose K_rel: min(n, max(10, int(0.004*(2n)))? for n 3000 =>24; 8000=>64. Cap 80
       K = min(n, max(10, int(0.004*(2*n))) )
       # top idx
       if K < b.size: idx=np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:]
       else idx=np.arange(n*2-1)
       idx=idx[np.isfinite(b[idx])]
       A=[] maybe dense list
       rows = []
       for k in idx:
           lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
           if lo>hi: continue
           row=np.zeros(n); row[lo:hi+1]=p[k-hi:k-lo+1][::-1]; rows.append(row)
       Arow=np.ones(n)
       b_ub = np.array([M]*len(rows)+[5.0*S])
       A=np.vstack(rows+[Arow])
       c=-np.ones(n)
       res=linprog(c,A_ub=A,b_ub=b_ub,bounds=(0,None),method='highs')
       if not res.success or res.x is None: break
       y=res.x; ssum=y.sum(); if ssum<1e-9: break
       y=(y/max(ssum,1e-12))*S; y=np.maximum(y,0)
       accepted=False
       for t in [0.25,0.15,0.08,0.04,0.02,0.01]:
           p_try=(1-t)*p+t*y
           # p_try already sum S if p,y sum S; but numerical normalize
           b_try=conv_full(p_try); sc_try=local_score(p_try,b_try)
           if sc_try < sc - 1e-9:
               p=p_try; sc=sc_try; accepted=True
               if sc < best_score: best_score=sc; best_seq=p.copy()
               break
       if not accepted:
           # maybe try smaller t 0.005?
           break
    return best_seq,best_score
```

_LBFGS_N = 0
_LBFGS_TAU = 1.0
_LBFGS_END = 0.0
_LBFGS_T0 = 0.0
_LBFGS_BEST_THETA = None
_LBFGS_BEST_VAL = np.inf
_LBFGS_LAST_THETA = None

class TimeLimit(Exception): pass

def _lbfgs_obj(theta):
    global ...
    if time.monotonic() > _LBFGS_END - 0.5: raise TimeLimit
    n=_LBFGS_N
    tmax=theta.max(); p=np.exp(theta-tmax); p/=p.sum()
    b=conv_full(p)
    bmax=b.max()
    tau=_LBFGS_TAU
    z=(b-bmax)/tau
    ex=np.exp(z-z.max()); s=ex.sum()
    val=2.0*n*tau*(bmax/tau + np.log(s))
    # store last/best
    if val < _LBFGS_BEST_VAL:
       _LBFGS_BEST_VAL=val; _LBFGS_BEST_THETA=theta.copy()
    # maybe if time...
    return float(val)

def _lbfgs_grad(theta):
    global ...
    if time > end raise
    tmax=theta.max(); p=...
    b=conv_full(p); bmax=...; ex... q=...; gp=2*corr_valid(q,p); mean=float(np.dot(p,gp)); return p*(gp-mean)
```

But θ gradient = p*(gp - mean) scaling by 2n constant; can omit constant for direction. L-BFGS line search objective scale influences trust but okay if omitted? Gradient must match function for L-BFGS. Need include consistent. If function val includes 2nτ..., gradient should include 2n*2corr. Let's include `gp = (2.0*n) * corr_valid(q,p)`? Derivative of `2n τ logsum(b/τ)`: 2n * 2corr(q,p) = 4n corr. Derivative of logsumexp: q normalized; dlogsum/dp = (1/τ)Σ exp db = q db? yes db derivative =2corr(q,p); so derivative = 2corr. So `gp = 4*n*corr(q,p)`. If q sum1. Use that.
Then θ_grad = p*(gp - p·gp) because softmax. Good.

def adam_run(p0,budget,end_limit,best_seq,best_score,polish=False,tag=""):
    if budget<=0: return best_seq,best_score
    p_init=np.asarray(p0,float).ravel()
    n=p_init.size
    if n<=1: ...
    S=math.sqrt(2.0*n)
    p=normalize_to_sum(p_init,S)
    best_p = p.copy() if best_seq is None else np.asarray(best_seq,float).ravel()
    best_sc = best_score if not None else np.inf
    if best_score <=? local score? Maybe ensure best_p sum S? Not necessary.
    # compute initial score to update best
    b=conv_full(p); sc=local_score(p,b); if sc<best_sc: best...
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); ad_t=0
    start=time.monotonic()
    base_lr = (2.0 if polish else 1.0) * S / n? Wait polish smaller: 0.4; balance 0.8.
    base_lr = (0.4 if polish else 0.9) * S / n
    lr_min = 0.01 * S / n
    beta1=0.8; beta2=0.95
    stagnant=0; last_print=start
    while True:
       now=time.monotonic()
       if now > end_limit - 1.0: break
       f=min(1.0,(now-start)/max(budget,1e-6))
       b=conv_full(p)
       sc=local_score(p,b)
       improved=False
       if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
           best_sc=sc; best_p=p.copy(); stagnant=0; improved=True
       else:
           stagnant += 1
       alpha_l2 = ...
       alpha_top = ...
       frac = ...
       w=weights_from_b(b,f,alpha_l2,alpha_top,frac)
       g=corr_valid(w,p)
       ad_t +=1
       m = beta1*m + (1-beta1)*g
       v = beta2*v + (1-beta2)*(g*g)
       mh=m/(1-beta1**ad_t)
       vh=v/(1-beta2**ad_t)
       lr = base_lr * (0.2 + 0.8*(1-f)**0.7)
       if stagnant > 300: lr=max(lr_min, lr*0.9)
       if stagnant > 1500:
           # perturb
           rho=np.std(best_p)
           if rho < S/n: rho=S/n
           rng=np.random.default_rng(12345+ad_t)
           p=best_p + rng.standard_normal(n)*0.03*rho
           p=project_simplex_sum(p,S)
           m.fill(0); v.fill(0); ad_t=0; stagnant=1000
           continue
       step = lr * mh / (np.sqrt(vh)+1e-8)
       max_step = 2.0*S/n if polish else 5.0*S/n
       step = np.clip(step, -max_step, max_step)
       p = project_simplex_sum(p - step, S)
       if now-last_print>10:
           print(f"{tag} f={f:.2f} score={sc:.6f} best={best_sc:.6f} n={n}")
           last_print=now
    # final score? b already maybe; if not compute
    return best_p.copy(), best_sc
```

```python
"""
Hybrid optimizer for minimizing 2*n*max(conv(a,a))/sum(a)^2.

Uses approximate convex/active-set intuition:
  - FFT-based convolution/correlation.
  - Adam-style projected updates driven by:
      * a variance-flattening direction (current convolution coefficients
        centered around their mean),
      * an active-max direction (top K convolution coefficients).
  - Periodic linear-cone update inspired by the old SOTA LP relaxation:
      solve a small LP for a nonnegative direction g such that
      (f*g)_k <= M on the most important constraints.
  - A final L-BFGS polish on a softmax parameterization of the simplex.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math

try:
    from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft, next_fast_len
except Exception:
    from numpy.fft import rfft, irfft

    def next_fast_len(n):
        m = 1
        while m < n:
            m *= 2
        return m

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize as _scipy_minimize, linprog as _scipy_linprog
except Exception:
    _scipy_minimize = None
    _scipy_linprog = None

# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# FFT helpers
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------

def conv_full(a):
    """Full linear convolution a * a using FFT for moderately large n."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([0.0], dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= 1024:
        b = np.convolve(a, a, mode="full")
    else:
        L = next_fast_len(2 * n)
        fa = rfft(a, L)
        b = irfft(fa * fa, L)[:2 * n - 1]
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    # FFT roundoff can create tiny negatives; coefficients are mathematically >= 0.
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    return b


def corr_valid(w, p):
    """
    Return c_i = sum_j w_{i+j} p_j, i=0..n-1, where w has length 2n-1.
    This is the un-scaled derivative wrapper for quadratic convolution forms.
    """
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= 1024:
        return np.asarray(np.correlate(w, p, mode="valid"), dtype=np.float64)
    L = next_fast_len(3 * n)
    W = rfft(w, L)
    P = rfft(p, L)
    c = irfft(W * np.conj(P), L)[:n]
    return np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)


# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Simplex utilities
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------

def normalize_to_sum(a, S):
    """Fast nonnegative normalization to sum S."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    n = a.size
    if s <= 1e-300 or n == 0:
        if n == 0:
            return a
        return np.full(n, S / n, dtype=np.float64)
    return a * (S / s)


def project_simplex_sum(v, S):
    """
    Euclidean projection onto {x >= 0, sum(x) = S}.
    Uses a bisection for the threshold; robust enough for optimization.
    """
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = v.size
    if n == 0:
        return v
    if S <= 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    # s(theta) = sum max(v - theta, 0) is decreasing.
    s0 = float(np.maximum(v, 0.0).sum())
    if s0 >= S:
        lo = 0.0
        hi = float(v.max()) if v.max() > 0 else 0.0
    else:
        lo = float(v.min()) - S - 1.0
        hi = 0.0

    for _ in range(36):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s_mid = float(np.maximum(v - mid, 0.0).sum())
        if s_mid > S:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid

    theta = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
    res = np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)

    diff = S - float(res.sum())
    if abs(diff) > 1e-12:
        idx = int(np.argmax(res))
        res[idx] += diff

    # Very rare fallback if rounding makes a component negative.
    if res[idx] < 0 or float(res.sum()) <= 0:
        res = np.maximum(res, 0.0)
        sres = float(res.sum())
        if sres <= 0:
            return np.full(n, S / n, dtype=np.float64)
        return res * (S / sres)
    return res


def local_score(p, b):
    """Objective value for a nonnegative sequence p and b=convolve(p,p)."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = p.size
    s = float(p.sum())
    if n == 0 or s <= 1e-300 or b.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    m = float(np.max(b))
    return float(2.0 * n * m / (s * s))


# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Direction/gradient weights
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------

def weights_from_b(b, f, alpha_l2, alpha_top, frac_top):
    """
    Build a unit-norm direction in coefficient space:
      - L2/variance flattening: centered coefficients.
      - active max: top K coefficients.
    """
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    m = b.size
    if m == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)

    c = b - float(b.mean())
    nc = float(np.linalg.norm(c))
    if nc > 1e-14:
        wl = c / nc
    else:
        wl = np.zeros_like(b)

    K = int(max(1, min(m, float(m) * max(1e-6, frac_top))))
    if K >= m:
        idx = np.arange(m)
    else:
        idx = np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:]
    wt = np.zeros_like(b)
    wt[idx] = 1.0
    nw = float(np.linalg.norm(wt))
    if nw > 1e-14:
        wt = wt / nw
    else:
        wt = np.zeros_like(b)

    w = alpha_l2 * wl + alpha_top * wt
    nw = float(np.linalg.norm(w))
    if nw > 1e-12:
        return w / nw
    if nc > 1e-14:
        return wl
    return wt


# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Initialization
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------

def load_provided():
    """Load height_sequence_1 if present, otherwise None."""
    try:
        raw = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()  # noqa: F821
    except Exception:
        return None
    if raw.size == 0:
        return None
    raw = np.where(np.isfinite(raw), raw, 0.0)
    raw = np.maximum(raw, 0.0)
    if float(raw.sum()) <= 1e-300:
        return None
    if raw.size > 10000:
        x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, raw.size)
        x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, 10000)
        raw = np.maximum(np.interp(x_new, x_old, raw), 0.0)
    return raw


def resample_sequence(arr, n):
    arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == n:
        return arr.copy()
    if arr.size <= 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    x0 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, arr.size)
    x1 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    return np.maximum(np.interp(x1, x0, arr), 0.0)


def init_uniform(n):
    return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)


def init_edge_power(n, a_left, a_right):
    """
    Mixture of endpoint singularities:
       p_i ~ (i+1)^(-a_left) * (n-i)^(-a_right).
    """
    n = int(n)
    x = np.arange(1.0, n + 1.0)
    left = x
    right = n - x + 1.0
    v = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if a_left != 0.0:
        v *= np.power(left, -float(a_left))
    if a_right != 0.0:
        v *= np.power(right, -float(a_right))
    return np.maximum(v, 1e-12)


def init_sinusoidal(n, a1, a2, b1, b2):
    """Bounded positive initialization from low-frequency sine/cosine modes."""
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    v = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if a1 != 0.0:
        v += float(a1) * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x)
    if a2 != 0.0:
        v += float(a2) * np.sin(4.0 * np.pi * x)
    if b1 != 0.0:
        v += float(b1) * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x)
    if b2 != 0.0:
        v += float(b2) * np.cos(4.0 * np.pi * x)
    return np.maximum(v, 0.05)


def init_random_exp(n, seed=1):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    return np.maximum(rng.exponential(1.0, size=n), 1e-12)


def make_initial(n, kind, params, prov=None):
    n = int(n)
    if kind == "prov":
        if prov is None or prov.size == 0:
            return init_uniform(n)
        return resample_sequence(prov, n)
    if kind == "uniform":
        return init_uniform(n)
    if kind == "edge":
        if params is None:
            return init_edge_power(n, 0.5, 0.2)
        return init_edge_power(n, float(params[0]), float(params[1]))
    if kind == "sine":
        if params is None:
            return init_sinusoidal(n, 0.8, 0.3, 0.0, 0.0)
        return init_sinusoidal(n, *params)
    if kind == "rand":
        seed = 12345
        if params is not None and len(params) > 0:
            seed = int(params[0])
        return init_random_exp(n, seed)
    return init_uniform(n)


# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Main Adam-like projected search
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------

def adam_run(p0, budget, end_limit, best_seq, best_score, polish=False, tag=""):
    """
    Fast projected Adam-style search.
    Keeps local best for the current length and global best for any length.
    """
    budget = float(budget)
    if budget <= 0.5 or p0 is None:
        return best_seq, best_score

    p_init = np.asarray(p0, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = p_init.size
    if n <= 0:
        return best_seq, best_score

    S = math.sqrt(2.0 * n)
    p = normalize_to_sum(p_init, S)

    b = conv_full(p)
    sc = local_score(p, b)

    global_seq = None if best_seq is None else np.asarray(best_seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    global_sc = float(best_score) if best_score is not None and np.isfinite(best_score) else float("inf")

    local_seq = p.copy()
    local_sc = sc

    if global_seq is not None and global_seq.size == n and global_sc < local_sc:
        local_seq = global_seq.copy()
        local_sc = global_sc

        # Do not start from a globally better sequence without re-centering to sum S.
        if local_seq.size == n:
            local_seq = normalize_to_sum(local_seq, S)
            if p.mean() > 0:
                # blend to avoid discontinuity if lengths were resampled differently
                p = 0.7 * p + 0.3 * local_seq
                p = normalize_to_sum(p, S)

    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    ad_t = 0

    base_lr = (0.45 if polish else 0.95) * (S / float(n))
    lr_min = 0.01 * (S / float(n))
    beta1 = 0.80
    beta2 = 0.95

    stagnant = 0
    last_print = 0.0
    start = time.monotonic()

    while True:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= end_limit - 0.8:
            break

        f = min(1.0, max(0.0, (now - start) / max(budget, 1e-6)))

        b = conv_full(p)
        sc = local_score(p, b)

        if sc < local_sc - 1e-12:
            local_sc = sc
            local_seq = p.copy()
            stagnant = 0
        else:
            stagnant += 1

        if sc < global_sc - 1e-12:
            global_sc = sc
            global_seq = p.copy()

        # Schedule.  Quick runs finish with a relatively "polish-like" state.
        alpha_l2 = 1.0 - 0.35 * f
        alpha_top = (0.9 + 1.6 * f) if polish else (0.35 + 1.35 * f)
        frac_top = (0.0006 + 0.0094 * (1.0 - f)) if polish else (0.0007 + 0.0133 * (1.0 - f))

        w = weights_from_b(b, f, alpha_l2, alpha_top, frac_top)
        g = corr_valid(w, p)

        ad_t += 1
        m = beta1 * m + (1.0 - beta1) * g
        v = beta2 * v + (1.0 - beta2) * (g * g)

        mh = m / (1.0 - beta1 ** ad_t)
        vh = v / (1.0 - beta2 ** ad_t)

        lr = base_lr * (0.18 + 0.82 * (1.0 - f) ** 0.75)
        if stagnant > 350:
            lr = max(lr_min, lr * 0.92)
        if stagnant > 1600:
            # Occasional escape: perturb the currently best sequence of this length.
            rho = float(np.std(local_seq))
            if rho <= 0:
                rho = S / float(n)
            rng = np.random.default_rng(900000 + ad_t)
            p = local_seq + rng.standard_normal(n) * (0.035 * rho)
            p = project_simplex_sum(p, S)
            m.fill(0.0)
            v.fill(0.0)
            ad_t = 0
            stagnant = 800
            continue

        step = lr * mh / (np.sqrt(vh) + 1e-9)
        max_step = (2.0 if polish else 5.0) * (S / float(n))
        step = np.clip(step, -max_step, max_step)

        p = project_simplex_sum(p - step, S)

        if now - last_print > 12.0:
            print(f"  [{tag}] n={n} f={f:.2f} cur={sc:.6f} local_best={local_sc:.6f} global_best={global_sc:.6f}")
            last_print = now

    # Score final point one more time.
    b = conv_full(p)
    sc = local_score(p, b)
    if sc < local_sc - 1e-14:
        local_sc = sc
        local_seq = p.copy()
    if sc < global_sc - 1e-14:
        global_sc = sc
        global_seq = p.copy()

    if local_sc <= global_sc:
        return local_seq.copy(), float(local_sc)
    if global_seq is None:
        return local_seq.copy(), float(local_sc)
    return global_seq.copy(), float(global_sc)


# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# LP-cone update, inspired by the previous SOTA
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------

def lp_update_sequence(p0, budget, end_limit, best_seq, best_score, tag=""):
    """
    A few active-set linear directions:
      maximize sum(g)
      subject to (p*g)_k <= M for the largest current convolution coefficients.
    Then mix in the direction with line search.
    """
    budget = float(budget)
    if budget <= 5.0 or p0 is None or _scipy_linprog is None:
        return best_seq, best_score

    p_init = np.asarray(p0, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = p_init.size
    if n <= 0:
        return best_seq, best_score

    S = math.sqrt(2.0 * n)
    p = normalize_to_sum(p_init, S)

    global_seq = None if best_seq is None else np.asarray(best_seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    global_sc = float(best_score) if best_score is not None and np.isfinite(best_score) else float("inf")

    b = conv_full(p)
    sc = local_score(p, b)
    local_seq = p.copy()
    local_sc = sc

    if global_seq is not None and global_seq.size == n and global_sc < local_sc:
        local_seq = normalize_to_sum(global_seq, S).copy()
        local_sc = global_sc

    if sc < global_sc - 1e-12:
        global_sc = sc
        global_seq = p.copy()

    no_improve = 0
    max_rounds = 12
    start = time.monotonic()

    for rnd in range(max_rounds):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= end_limit - 2.0 or now - start > budget:
            break

        b = conv_full(p)
        sc = local_score(p, b)
        if sc < local_sc - 1e-12:
            local_sc = sc
            local_seq = p.copy()
        if sc < global_sc - 1e-12:
            global_sc = sc
            global_seq = p.copy()

        M = float(np.max(b))
        if M <= 1e-14:
            break

        K = int(max(10, min(50, max(10, int(0.004 * (2 * n)))))
        K = min(K, b.size)
        if K >= b.size:
            idx = np.arange(b.size)
        else:
            idx = np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:]

        rows = []
        for k in idx:
            lo = max(0, int(k) - n + 1)
            hi = min(n - 1, int(k))
            if lo > hi:
                continue
            row = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            # coefficient of y_j is p_{k-j}
            row[lo:hi + 1] = p[k - hi:k - lo + 1][::-1]
            rows.append(row)

        if len(rows) == 0:
            break

        sum_row = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        A_list = rows + [sum_row]
        A = np.vstack(A_list)
        bub = np.concatenate([np.full(len(rows), M, dtype=np.float64),
                              np.array([8.0 * S], dtype=np.float64)])

        ub = 100.0 * S / float(n)
        c = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

        try:
            res = _scipy_linprog(
                c,
                A_ub=A,
                b_ub=bub,
                bounds=(0.0, ub),
                method="highs",
            )
        except Exception:
            try:
                res = _scipy_linprog(
                    c,
                    A_ub=A,
                    b_ub=bub,
                    bounds=[(0.0, ub)] * n,
                    method="highs",
                )
            except Exception:
                break

        if res is None or not getattr(res, "success", False):
            break
        y = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
        if y.size != n:
            break
        y = np.maximum(y, 0.0)
        sy = float(y.sum())
        if sy <= 1e-14:
            break
        y = y * (S / sy)

        accepted = False
        for t in (0.25, 0.15, 0.08, 0.04, 0.02, 0.01, 0.005):
            p_try = (1.0 - t) * p + t * y
            p_try = normalize_to_sum(p_try, S)
            b_try = conv_full(p_try)
            sc_try = local_score(p_try, b_try)
            if sc_try < sc - 1e-10:
                p = p_try
                sc = sc_try
                accepted = True
                no_improve = 0

                if sc < local_sc - 1e-12:
                    local_sc = sc
                    local_seq = p.copy()
                if sc < global_sc - 1e-12:
                    global_sc = sc
                    global_seq = p.copy()
                print(f"  [{tag}] LP round {rnd+1} improved to {sc:.6f}")
                break
        if not accepted:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve >= 2:
                break

    if local_sc <= global_sc:
        return local_seq.copy(), float(local_sc)
    if global_seq is None:
        return local_seq.copy(), float(local_sc)
    return global_seq.copy(), float(global_sc)


# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# L-BFGS polish
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------

class TimeLimit(Exception):
    pass


_LBFGS_N = 0
_LBFGS_TAU = 1e-12
_LBFGS_END = 0.0
_LBFGS_BEST_THETA = None
_LBFGS_BEST_VAL = float("inf")


def _softmax_stable(theta):
    tmax = float(theta.max())
    p = np.exp(theta - tmax)
    p = np.maximum(p, 1e-300)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 0:
        p = np.ones_like(theta)
        s = float(p.sum())
    return p / s


def _lbfgs_obj(theta):
    global _LBFGS_BEST_THETA, _LBFGS_BEST_VAL
    if time.monotonic() > _LBFGS_END - 0.5:
        raise TimeLimit

    n = _LBFGS_N
    p = _softmax_stable(theta)
    b = conv_full(p)
    bmax = float(np.max(b))
    tau = max(_LBFGS_TAU, 1e-18)

    z = (b - bmax) / tau
    e = np.exp(z - float(z.max()))
    sz = float(e.sum())
    if sz <= 0 or not np.isfinite(sz):
        val = float(np.inf)
    else:
        val = float(2.0 * n * (bmax + tau * np.log(sz)))

    if val < _LBFGS_BEST_VAL:
        _LBFGS_BEST_VAL = val
        _LBFGS_BEST_THETA = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64).copy()

    return val


def _lbfgs_grad(theta):
    if time.monotonic() > _LBFGS_END - 0.5:
        raise TimeLimit

    n = _LBFGS_N
    p = _softmax_stable(theta)
    b = conv_full(p)
    bmax = float(np.max(b))
    tau = max(_LBFGS_TAU, 1e-18)

    z = (b - bmax) / tau
    e = np.exp(z - float(z.max()))
    sz = float(e.sum())
    if sz <= 0 or not np.isfinite(sz):
        return np.zeros_like(p)
    q = e / sz

    # d/dp of 2*n*tau*logsumexp(b/tau) = 2*n*2*corr_valid(q,p).
    gp = (4.0 * float(n)) * corr_valid(q, p)
    mean = float(np.dot(p, gp))
    return p * (gp - mean)


def _lbfgs_callback(xk):
    if time.monotonic() > _LBFGS_END - 0.5:
        raise TimeLimit
    return None


def lbfgs_polish(p0, budget, end_limit, best_seq, best_score, tag=""):
    """Softmax-parameterized L-BFGS on the simplex."""
    budget = float(budget)
    if budget <= 20.0 or p0 is None or _scipy_minimize is None:
        return best_seq, best_score

    p_init = np.asarray(p0, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = p_init.size
    if n <= 0 or n > 9000:
        return best_seq, best_score

    S = math.sqrt(2.0 * n)
    p = p_init.copy()
    ps = float(p.sum())
    if ps <= 1e-300:
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        ps = float(n)
    p = p / ps

    b = conv_full(p)
    bmax = float(np.max(b))
    base_score = local_score(p, b)

    tau = 0.12 * max(bmax, 1e-12)

    theta0 = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
    theta0 = theta0 - float(theta0.mean())

    global _LBFGS_N, _LBFGS_TAU, _LBFGS_END, _LBFGS_BEST_THETA, _LBFGS_BEST_VAL
    _LBFGS_N = n
    _LBFGS_TAU = float(tau)
    _LBFGS_END = min(end_limit, time.monotonic() + budget)
    _LBFGS_BEST_THETA = theta0.copy()
    _LBFGS_BEST_VAL = float("inf")

    maxiter = int(max(30, min(350, budget * 1.8)))
    maxfun = int(max(60, min(900, budget * 3.5)))

    try:
        _scipy_minimize(
            _lbfgs_obj,
            theta0,
            jac=_lbfgs_grad,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            callback=_lbfgs_callback,
            options={
                "maxiter": maxiter,
                "maxfun": maxfun,
                "gtol": 1e-10,
                "ftol": 1e-12,
            },
        )
    except Exception:
        pass

    if _LBFGS_BEST_THETA is not None:
        theta = np.asarray(_LBFGS_BEST_THETA, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        theta = theta0

    p = _softmax_stable(theta)

    # Convert back to the normalization used elsewhere.
    best_p = normalize_to_sum(p, S)
    b = conv_full(best_p)
    sc = local_score(best_p, b)

    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < base_score - 1e-10:
        print(f"  [{tag}] L-BFGS improved: {base_score:.6f} -> {sc:.6f}")
        if sc < best_score:
            return best_p.copy(), float(sc)
    return best_seq, best_score


# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Post-processing
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------

def try_trim_endpoints(seq, S):
    """Trim extremely small end points if beneficial."""
    arr = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = arr.size
    if n < 100:
        return seq
    avg = float(arr.mean())
    eps = 1e-10 * max(1.0, avg)
    l = 0
    r = n
    max_cut = max(1, n // 20)

    while l < min(max_cut, n - 50) and l < r and arr[l] <= eps:
        l += 1
    while (n - r) < max_cut and r < max_cut + (n - r) and r > l + 50 and arr[r - 1] <= eps:
        r -= 1

    trial = arr[l:r].copy()
    if trial.size < max(50, n // 2):
        return seq

    # Scale does not affect score, but trim changes n and sum.
    s = float(trial.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return seq
    b = conv_full(trial)
    tr_sc = local_score(trial, b)

    b_full = conv_full(arr)
    full_sc = local_score(arr, b_full)

    if np.isfinite(tr_sc) and tr_sc < full_sc - 1e-9:
        return trial
    return seq


# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Top-level required function
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------

def propose_candidate():
    """Search for a low-score nonnegative step-height sequence."""
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + 2360.0  # leave ~40s for final evaluation/return.

    prov = load_provided()
    best_seq = None
    best_score = float("inf")

    # Establish a baseline immediately.
    if prov is not None:
        n_base = int(prov.size)
        S_base = math.sqrt(2.0 * n_base)
        p_base = normalize_to_sum(prov, S_base)
        b_base = conv_full(p_base)
        s_base = local_score(p_base, b_base)
        if np.isfinite(s_base):
            best_seq = p_base.copy()
            best_score = float(s_base)
        print(f"baseline provided: n={n_base}, score={best_score:.6f}")
    else:
        p_base = normalize_to_sum(init_uniform(3000), math.sqrt(6000.0))
        b_base = conv_full(p_base)
        best_seq = p_base.copy()
        best_score = float(local_score(p_base, b_base))
        print(f"baseline uniform 3000: score={best_score:.6f}")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Quick diverse warmup
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    tasks = []
    seen = set()

    def add_task(n, kind, params=None):
        key = (int(n), str(kind), tuple(params) if params is not None else ())
        n = int(max(100, min(int(n), 10000)))
        if key in seen:
            return
        seen.add(key)
        tasks.append((n, kind, params))

    if prov is not None:
        add_task(prov.size, "prov")
        add_task(5000, "prov")
        add_task(8000, "prov")

    add_task(3000, "uniform")
    add_task(8000, "uniform")
    add_task(3000, "edge", (0.5, 0.2))
    add_task(3000, "edge", (0.75, 0.0))
    add_task(5000, "edge", (0.5, 0.1))
    add_task(3000, "sine", (0.8, 0.3, 0.0, 0.0))
    add_task(3000, "rand", (7))
    add_task(10000, "uniform")

    quick_end = min(deadline - 500.0, t0 + 180.0)
    n_tasks = len(tasks)
    if n_tasks > 0:
        t_per = max(6.0, (quick_end - time.monotonic()) / float(n_tasks))
        print(f"\nquick warmup: {n_tasks} tasks, about {t_per:.1f}s each")
        for i, (n, kind, params) in enumerate(tasks):
            remain_q = quick_end - time.monotonic()
            if remain_q < 8.0:
                break
            budget_i = min(t_per, remain_q)
            try:
                p_i = make_initial(n, kind, params, prov)
                p_i = normalize_to_sum(p_i, math.sqrt(2.0 * n))
                if i % 3 == 0 or i == n_tasks - 1:
                    print(f"  warmup {i+1}/{n_tasks} n={n} kind={kind}")
                best_seq, best_score = adam_run(
                    p_i, budget_i, quick_end,
                    best_seq, best_score,
                    polish=False, tag=f"warmup{i}"
                )
            except Exception as exc:
                print(f"  warmup task failed: {exc}")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Main long balance run
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    remain = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remain > 120 and best_seq is not None:
        budget1 = min(remain * 0.42, 950.0)
        if budget1 > 60:
            print(f"\nmain Adam balance: n={len(best_seq)} budget={budget1:.0f}s best={best_score:.6f}")
            best_seq, best_score = adam_run(
                best_seq, budget1, time.monotonic() + budget1,
                best_seq, best_score,
                polish=False, tag="main"
            )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # LP-cone active-set insertion
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    remain = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remain > 80 and best_seq is not None and best_seq.size <= 10000:
        budget_lp1 = min(remain * 0.10, 220.0)
        if budget_lp1 > 25:
            print(f"\nLP cone update: n={len(best_seq)} budget={budget_lp1:.0f}s best={best_score:.6f}")
            best_seq, best_score = lp_update_sequence(
                best_seq, budget_lp1, time.monotonic() + budget_lp1,
                best_seq, best_score, tag="lp1"
            )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Second Adam polish run
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    remain = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remain > 80 and best_seq is not None:
        budget2 = min(remain * 0.35, 700.0)
        if budget2 > 45:
            print(f"\nAdam polish: n={len(best_seq)} budget={budget2:.0f}s best={best_score:.6f}")
            best_seq, best_score = adam_run(
                best_seq, budget2, time.monotonic() + budget2,
                best_seq, best_score,
                polish=True, tag="polish"
            )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # L-BFGS final local refinement
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    remain = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remain > 90 and best_seq is not None and best_seq.size <= 9000:
        budget_lb = min(remain * 0.45, 420.0)
        if budget_lb > 50:
            print(f"\nL-BFGS polish: n={len(best_seq)} budget={budget_lb:.0f}s best={best_score:.6f}")
            best_seq, best_score = lbfgs_polish(
                best_seq, budget_lb, time.monotonic() + budget_lb,
                best_seq, best_score, tag="lbfgs"
            )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Final LP + tiny Adam top-up
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    remain = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remain > 45 and best_seq is not None and best_seq.size <= 10000:
        budget_lp2 = min(remain * 0.40, 120.0)
        if budget_lp2 > 20:
            best_seq, best_score = lp_update_sequence(
                best_seq, budget_lp2, time.monotonic() + budget_lp2,
                best_seq, best_score, tag="lp2"
            )

    remain = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remain > 25 and best_seq is not None:
        budget3 = min(remain * 0.80, 90.0)
        if budget3 > 12:
            best_seq, best_score = adam_run(
                best_seq, budget3, time.monotonic() + budget3,
                best_seq, best_score,
                polish=True, tag="final"
            )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Finalize / validate
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    if best_seq is None:
        best_seq = normalize_to_sum(init_uniform(3000), math.sqrt(6000.0))
        best_score = float(local_score(best_seq, conv_full(best_seq)))

    best_seq = np.asarray(best_seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    best_seq = np.maximum(best_seq, 0.0)

    # Trim tiny endpoint effects if helpful.
    best_seq = try_trim_endpoints(best_seq, math.sqrt(2.0 * best_seq.size))
    best_seq = np.asarray(best_seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    best_seq = np.maximum(best_seq, 0.0)

    # Safety caps: finite values, finite length, sum not too small.
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(best_seq)):
        best_seq = np.nan_to_num(best_seq, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)

    if best_seq.size > 10000:
        best_seq = resample_sequence(best_seq, 10000)

    s = float(best_seq.sum())
    if s <= 1e-6:
        best_seq = np.ones(1000, dtype=np.float64)
    elif s < 0.01:
        best_seq = best_seq * (1.0 / s)

    out = [float(x) for x in best_seq]

    try:
        ev = evaluate_sequence(out)  # noqa: F821
        if isinstance(ev, float) and np.isfinite(ev):
            # If the evaluator sees a slightly different value due to scaling/clipping,
            # store it for logging.  We still return the same sequence.
            print(f"\nFINAL returned local best ~{best_score:.6f}, evaluator says {ev:.6f}, n={len(out)}")
        else:
            print(f"\nFINAL returned local best ~{best_score:.6f}, evaluator returned non-finite, n={len(out)}")
    except Exception as exc:
        print(f"\nFINAL evaluation error: {exc}, returning local best n={len(out)}")

    return out
```
